# SOURCES

Every source used by any agent in this engagement, merged from `research/sources/*.jsonl` by `research/tools/merge_sources.py`, deduplicated by URL. Accessed dates are 2026-10/11 unless stated. Types: primary-filing / company-IR / government / peer-reviewed / conference / patent are primary; market-research is usually a free press release about a paywalled report; news/blog are secondary.

**Total unique sources: 1613**
